EBFT augments the conventional Byzantine fault bound with two separate, confidence-indexed quantities: $e_δ$ bounds coherent invalid endorsements outside the Byzantine set, and $u_ε$ bounds unusable validator support that degrades liveness. These quantities characterize semantic safety risk and liveness degradation independently. We derive quorum-threshold conditions for semantic validity, consensus agreement, liveness, and feasible threshold selection, and outline a calibration methodology for estimating these budgets. We show that adding nominally distinct agents improves fault tolerance only when it measurably reduces the upper-tail concentration of invalid endorsements or unusable support.

</details>


### [98] [Improving Improved Kernel PLS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16138)
*Ole-Christian Galbo Engstrøm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对IKPLS算法中计算X旋转矩阵R和Y载荷矩阵Q的两个步骤进行加速：R采用直接求值策略以提升并行性，Q首次发现可从已有中间结果直接计算，将复杂度从Θ(KM)降至Θ(M)。加速后结果与原算法完全相同，在CPU和GPU上分别实现约2倍和6倍的整体加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IKPLS算法1和2是最快的PLS校准算法之一，但其计算X旋转矩阵R和Y载荷矩阵Q的两个步骤仍有优化空间，尤其是Q的计算复杂度较高（Θ(KM)），且R的计算并行性不佳。

Method: 对于R，将逐项累加替换为直接求值策略，保持相同乘法次数但提高并行性；对于Q，首次发现每个Y载荷可从同一迭代中已计算的中间量直接获得（仅需乘以显式导出的常数），将复杂度从Θ(KM)降至Θ(M)（当M=1或2≤M<K时）。

Result: 两种改进在数学上与原算法等价，保证输出W、P、Q、R、T完全相同。基于NumPy（CPU）和JAX（GPU）的基准测试显示，孤立步骤加速可达两个数量级，整体拟合加速约2倍（CPU）和6倍（GPU）。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持计算结果完全一致的前提下显著提升了IKPLS算法效率，并已集成到开源Python包ikpls中供社区使用。

Abstract: Improved Kernel Partial Least Squares (IKPLS) algorithms 1 and 2 are among the fastest PLS calibration algorithms. This article focuses on two shared steps, the computation of the $\mathbf{X}$ rotations, $\mathbf{R}$, and the $\mathbf{Y}$ loadings, $\mathbf{Q}$, and accelerates both. For $\mathbf{R}$, term-by-term accumulation is replaced by a direct evaluation strategy that requires the same number of multiplications but parallelizes better on modern hardware. For $\mathbf{Q}$, I identify - to the best of my knowledge, for the first time - equivalences showing that each $\mathbf{Y}$ loading is obtainable, up to explicitly derived constants, from quantities already computed earlier in the same iteration, and I exploit them in IKPLS to reduce the cost of each loading from $Θ\left(KM\right)$ to $Θ\left(M\right)$ operations whenever $M = 1$ or $2 \leq M < K$, with $K$ predictor variables (number of columns in $\mathbf{X}$) and $M$ response variables (number of columns in $\mathbf{Y}$). Both improvements provably yield exactly the same $\mathbf{W}$, $\mathbf{P}$, $\mathbf{Q}$, $\mathbf{R}$, and $\mathbf{T}$ as the original algorithms. Benchmarks with NumPy (CPU) and JAX (GPU) show speedups of up to two orders of magnitude for the isolated steps and of approximately $2\times$ (CPU) and $6\times$ (GPU) for entire fits. Both improvements are implemented in the free, open-source Python package \texttt{ikpls}.

</details>


### [99] [PRISA: Proactive Infrastructure LiDAR Framework for Intersection Safety Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16156)
*Tam Bang,Hussam Abubakr,Emiliano de la Garza Villarreal,Truc Phuong Nguyen,Austin Harris,Toru Hirano,Mina Sartipi,Yunfei Xu,Hoang H. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRISA是一个模块化基础设施LiDAR框架，利用隐私保护、低光鲁棒的路边传感器进行长期交通观测和边缘实时风险检测。它包含感知层和即插即用的风险评估模块，后者自动从感知输出中整理训练数据以训练轨迹预测模型，并部署模型进行连续运动预测和双重代理安全评估（TTC用于纵向冲突，PPET用于交叉和弱势道路用户交互）。在R-LiViT数据集和Chattanooga实际信号交叉口部署评估，PPET评估端到端延迟194ms（2.4秒预测范围），TTC检测和感知满足实时约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交叉口是道路网络中最危险的地点之一，对车辆和行人、自行车等弱势道路用户构成重大风险。多智能体交互的复杂性需要持续、实时的监控系统，能够在冲突升级为碰撞之前进行预测。

Method: 提出模块化基础设施LiDAR框架PRISA，包含两大组件：(1) 感知与感知层；(2) 即插即用的风险评估模块。该模块自动从累积的感知输出中整理站点特定的训练数据，无需人工标注即可训练轨迹预测模型，然后部署模型进行连续运动预测和双重代理安全评估（TTC用于纵向冲突，PPET用于交叉和VRU交互）。

Result: 在R-LiViT数据集和Chattanooga实际信号交叉口（部署于NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor）评估。PPET评估在2.4秒预测范围内端到端延迟为194ms，TTC检测和感知满足实时约束。

Conclusion: PRISA展示了在边缘设备上实现主动式多智能体交叉口安全监控的实际可行性。

Abstract: Urban intersections are among the most hazardous locations in road networks, posing significant risks to vehicles and vulnerable road users (VRUs) such as pedestrians and cyclists. The complexity of multi-agent interactions demands continuous, real-time monitoring systems capable of anticipating conflicts before they escalate into crashes. We present PRISA, a modular infrastructure LiDAR framework leveraging privacy-preserving, low-light-robust roadside sensors for long-term traffic observation and real-time risk detection at the edge. The framework comprises two core components: a sensing and perception layer and a plug-and-play risk assessment module. The latter automatically curates site-specific training data from accumulated perception outputs to train a trajectory prediction model without manual annotation. It then deploys the trained model for continuous motion forecasting and dual surrogate safety evaluation, using Time-to-Collision (TTC) for longitudinal conflicts and Predicted Post-Encroachment Time (PPET) for crossing and VRU-involved interactions. PRISA is evaluated on the public R-LiViT dataset and deployed on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor at a live signalized intersection in Chattanooga, Tennessee. PPET-based assessment operates at 194~ms end-to-end latency over a 2.4-second predictive horizon, with TTC-based detection and perception remaining within real-time constraints, demonstrating practical feasibility for proactive multi-agent intersection safety monitoring.

</details>


### [100] [Behaviour-Conditioned Neural Processes for Adaptive Residential Short-Term Load Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16168)
*Ramin Soleimani,Andrea Visentin,Dirk Pesch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种行为条件化的注意力神经过程框架，通过将推断出的行为结构嵌入概率预测模型中，实现面向异质性家庭居民的场景条件化短期负荷预测，实验表明该方法在MAE和CRPS指标上优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭短期负荷预测面临负荷分布异质、时间变化大且受多样化行为模式影响等挑战。现有方法仅将行为结构作为外部分组信号，未嵌入预测模型内部，限制了模型对行为信息的利用效率。

Method: 提出行为条件化注意力神经过程框架：1) 离散隐变量表征行为结构，从上下文推断并用于解码器条件化；2) 连续隐变量捕获跨异质曲线的共享函数不确定性；3) 弱监督训练利用聚类信息代替真实行为标签，测试时仅依赖上下文推断的类别分布。

Result: 在SGSC数据集上，所提变体在所有预测步长和上下文设置下均优于标签无关的ANP基线，在有限上下文中增益最大。最佳变体相比ANP平均降低MAE 7.9%、CRPS 6.9%。相比固定窗口基线，在所有预测步长上RMSE更低。

Conclusion: 将行为结构嵌入神经过程框架可有效提升异质家庭、不同上下文和预测步长下的单模型、不确定性感知预测性能。

Abstract: Residential short-term load forecasting (STLF) is challenging because household demand is heterogeneous, temporally variable, and shaped by diverse behavioural routines. This work investigates whether inferred behavioural structure can be embedded within the forecasting mechanism of a Neural Process-based probabilistic model, rather than used only as an external grouping signal, for context-conditioned residential STLF. We propose a behaviour-conditioned Attentive Neural Process framework that treats each load profile as a forecasting task. Behavioural structure is represented by a discrete latent variable inferred from the available context and used for behaviour-conditioned decoder conditioning, while a continuous latent variable captures shared functional uncertainty across heterogeneous profiles. To enable conditioning without ground-truth behavioural labels, clustering-derived information provides weak supervision during training, whereas test-time conditioning relies only on context-inferred class distributions. Experiments on the Smart Grid, Smart City (SGSC) dataset use user-disjoint train/validation/test splits, variable context lengths, and multi-step forecast horizons, with comparisons against a label-agnostic ANP baseline and fixed-window deterministic STLF baselines. The proposed variants improve MAE and CRPS over ANP across horizons and context settings, with the largest gains under limited context. The best-performing variant achieves average reductions of 7.9% in MAE and 6.9% in CRPS relative to ANP. Compared with fixed-window baselines, this variant achieves lower RMSE across all evaluated horizons while maintaining competitive MAE, suggesting fewer large prediction deviations under heterogeneous consumption patterns. These results support single-model, uncertainty-aware forecasting across heterogeneous households, contexts, and horizons.

</details>


### [101] [A Blueprint for Equilibrium-Based Differentiable Continuous-Variable Thermodynamic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16183)
*Owen Lockwood,Jérémy Béjanin,Joost Bus,Christopher Chamberland,Patrick Huembeli,Frank Schäfer,Guillaume Verdon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于热力学计算的硬件蓝图，利用随机模拟过程（Langevin动力学）和可调能量势在物理硬件中实现能量基模型，通过概率图模型构建和训练机器学习模型，并基于随机模拟超导电路进行了初步实验验证，旨在实现高能效的概率机器学习硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器学习工作负载日益增长的能耗和延迟需求，探索利用物理硬件中的随机模拟过程实现高能效计算的新范式。

Method: 提出热力学计算架构蓝图，利用Langevin动力学描述的随机模拟过程在物理硬件中实现可调能量势；基于能量基模型通过概率图模型框架构建和训练机器学习模型；采用随机模拟超导电路作为硬件实验实现，通过热噪声驱动进行初步验证。

Result: 从理论分析和数值研究两方面分析了不同模型在热力学范式下的运行时和能耗表现；通过随机模拟超导电路实验初步验证了硬件实现的可行性。

Conclusion: 研究结果描绘了一条实现高能效热力学概率机器学习硬件的可行路径。

Abstract: To address the escalating energy and latency demands of machine-learning workloads, we introduce a blueprint for an energy-efficient and fast thermodynamic computing stack that leverages stochastic analog processes in physical hardware. In this work, we focus on energy-based thermodynamic computing where the stochastic process is well described by Langevin dynamics with tunable energy potentials. The implementation of such potentials in physical hardware enables us to generate and sample from basic parameterized energy-based models. We demonstrate how to construct and train popular classes of machine learning models based on these hardware-native energy-based models, using the framework of probabilistic graphical models. We analyze the runtime and energy consumption of different models in this thermodynamic paradigm based on theoretical considerations and numerical studies. As a preliminary experimental realization of such hardware, we present our stochastic analog superconducting circuits driven by thermal noise. Together, these results outline a path toward energy-efficient thermodynamic hardware for probabilistic machine learning.

</details>


### [102] [PagedWeight: Efficient MoE LLM Serving with Dynamic Quality-Aware Weight Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16184)
*Yuchen Yang,Yifan Zhao,Anisha Dasgupta,Sasa Misailovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PagedWeight是一种针对MoE大语言模型的新型动态运行时权重量化管理方法，通过权衡专家权重精度与KV缓存大小，在KV缓存密集型服务场景中实现GPU内存节省和吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在KV缓存密集型服务场景中，模型权重的GPU内存需求与不断增长的KV缓存之间存在矛盾，现有方法未能有效处理这一权衡。

Method: 提出PagedWeight方法，在运行时动态量化MoE模型的权重，并根据任务精度、内存消耗和吞吐量/延迟之间的复杂权衡，动态调整专家权重精度与KV缓存大小的分配。

Result: 在多个内存敏感的MoE服务场景中，相比现有量化基线，PagedWeight在保持FP16等效精度的同时，最高节省72.0%的GPU内存，吞吐量提升1.94倍；在相似内存预算下，质量相比量化方法提升最高39.3%，吞吐量损失最多不超过4.1%。

Conclusion: PagedWeight有效改善了MoE模型服务中的质量-内存权衡，是一种高效的动态量化管理方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) is a popular class of large language models (LLMs), offering high efficiency and accuracy. However, in KV-cache-intensive serving scenarios, MoEs often exhibit a tension between the GPU memory requirements of the model weights and the growing KV cache. We propose PagedWeight, a novel management method for MoE LLM serving that dynamically quantizes MoE model's weights at runtime and balances expert-weight precision with the KV cache sizes. PagedWeight exposes and effectively navigates the complex tradeoff between the model's task accuracy, memory consumption, and throughput/latency. Across several memory-sensitive MoE serving scenarios, PagedWeight improves the quality-memory tradeoff over several existing quantization baselines. PagedWeight achieves FP16-equivalent accuracy with up to 72.0% GPU memory savings and 1.94$\times$ throughput improvement, and improves quality over quantization methods by up to 39.3% at a similar memory budget with at most 4.1% throughput loss.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [On the Computational Complexity of Bilevel Integer Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15369)
*Nagisa Sugishita,Margarida Carvalho*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明双层整数线性规划的决策版本即使在一般整数变量下也是Σ^p_2-完全的，解决了Jeroslow (1985)遗留40余年的开放问题；并证明了当上下层变量总数固定时该问题可在多项式时间内求解，强化了Köppe等人(2010)的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jeroslow (1985)已证明二元变量情形下双层整数线性规划的决策问题是Σ^p_2-完全的，但一般整数变量下的复杂度问题40余年来一直悬而未决。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，并探索不同结构性假设对计算复杂度的影响。

Method: 采用计算复杂性理论分析，通过归约等方法证明Σ^p_2-完全性；同时利用固定参数可解性分析，考察变量总数固定时的多项式时间可解性。

Result: (1) 双层整数线性规划在一般整数变量下仍然是Σ^p_2-完全的，回答了40年开放问题；(2) 当上下层变量总数固定时（无需额外假设），该问题可在多项式时间内求解，强化了Köppe等人(2010)的结果。

Conclusion: 双层整数线性规划的复杂度在一般整数变量下保持了Σ^p_2-完全性，且固定变量总数是保证多项式时间可解性的充分条件。

Abstract: We investigate the computational complexity of bilevel integer linear programming. While Jeroslow~(1985) established that the decision version of this problem is $Σ^p_2$-complete when restricted to binary variables, we prove that this $Σ^p_2$-completeness persists even for general integer variables, settling a question that remained open for over 40 years. Furthermore, we analyze the impact of various structural assumptions on computational complexity. Notably, we strengthen the result of Köppe et al.~(2010) by proving polynomial-time solvability whenever the total number of upper- and lower-level variables is fixed, without any additional assumptions.

</details>


### [104] [Harnessing GPU Acceleration in Large-Scale Process Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15420)
*Boxun Huang,David Y. Shu,Michel Schanen,Mihai Anitescu,Rahul Gandhi,Sungho Shin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个完全在GPU上运行的面向方程的过程优化概念验证工作流，利用ExaModels、MadNLP和cuDSS构建GPU兼容的过程优化模型。在CO2吸收塔设计问题中，针对5000个场景和150万变量的最大案例，相比单线程CPU基线实现了约21倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程优化模型通常包含复杂的互联单元操作、动力学和不确定性，导致大规模非线性规划问题对传统CPU求解器计算负担沉重。尽管新兴GPU求解器具有显著计算优势，但由于缺乏GPU兼容的过程建模工具，其在过程优化中的应用受到限制。

Method: 使用GPU兼容的优化软件栈：ExaModels（代数建模系统）以GPU兼容方式表达问题，暴露重复代数结构；MadNLP（优化求解器）和cuDSS（线性求解器）在GPU上求解非线性规划问题。工作流在CO2吸收塔设计问题上进行验证，在进料不确定性下最小化共享塔径，并满足所有场景下的平衡和水力约束。

Result: 在5000个场景和150万变量的最大案例中，GPU工作流相比使用JuMP、Ipopt和MA57的单线程CPU基线实现了约21倍加速。

Conclusion: 该工作流展示了GPU在过程优化中的巨大潜力，弥合了GPU求解器与过程建模之间的差距，为大规模过程优化问题提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a proof-of-concept workflow for equation-oriented process optimization that runs entirely on a GPU. Process optimization models often incorporate complex interconnected unit operations, dynamics, and uncertainties, resulting in large nonlinear programs that can be computationally demanding for conventional CPU-based solvers. Although emerging GPU-based solvers offer substantial computational benefits, their application to process optimization has been limited by the lack of GPU-compatible process modeling tools. We address this gap by prototyping the GPU-compatible process optimization models using an existing GPU-capable optimization software stack, including ExaModels (algebraic modeling system), MadNLP (optimization solver), and cuDSS (linear solver). ExaModels formulates the process optimization problem in a GPU-compatible way by exposing its repeated algebraic structure, while MadNLP and cuDSS solve the resulting nonlinear program on the GPU. This workflow is demonstrated on a CO2 absorber design problem under feed uncertainty, in which a shared column diameter is minimized subject to equilibrium and hydraulic constraints in all scenarios. For the largest case with 5,000 scenarios and 1.5 million variables, the GPU workflow achieves a speedup of approximately 21\times over a single-threaded CPU baseline using JuMP, Ipopt, and MA57.

</details>


### [105] [A Unified Variational Framework for Optimal Transport with Lagrangian Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15471)
*Hailiang Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一变分框架，将经典最优传输理论（Monge-Kantorovich和Benamou-Brenier）推广到一般拉格朗日作用泛函，建立了拉格朗日、欧拉、凸优化、Hamilton-Jacobi对偶和Hamilton流五种等价表述，并揭示了Wasserstein空间上的自然哈密顿结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典二次代价最优传输理论推广到一般拉格朗日作用泛函，建立统一的变分框架，连接多种等价表述形式。

Method: 通过变分论证和凸对偶方法，在拉格朗日函数满足标准凸性假设下，证明多种表述的等价性，推导出Wasserstein空间上的哈密顿结构。

Result: 建立了包含拉格朗日、欧拉、凸优化、Hamilton-Jacobi对偶和Hamilton流五种等价表述的统一框架，得到了具有哈密顿结构的最优性系统。

Conclusion: 该框架将经典二次代价理论推广到一般拉格朗日代价，为作用泛函生成的传输度量提供了统一分析视角，并建立了最优传输、哈密顿动力学与最优控制之间的直接联系。

Abstract: We investigate optimal transport distances induced by general Lagrangian action functionals. Extending the classical Monge - Kantorovich and Benamou - Brenier theories, we derive a unified variational framework that connects several equivalent formulations of the induced transport distance, including Lagrangian, Eulerian, convex optimization, Hamilton - Jacobi dual, and Hamiltonian flow formulations. Under standard convexity assumptions on the Lagrangian, we establish the equivalence of these formulations through variational arguments and convex duality. The resulting optimality system reveals a natural Hamiltonian structure on the Wasserstein space, providing a direct link between optimal transport, Hamiltonian dynamics, and optimal control. The proposed framework extends the classical quadratic-cost theory to general Lagrangian costs and offers a unified perspective for the analysis of transport metrics generated by action functionals.

</details>


### [106] [Tropical Bi-Objective Pseudolinear Optimization as Parametric Mean-Payoff Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15481)
*Ibrahim Hassan,Abdulhadi Aminu,Lawal Muhammad*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将参数化平均收益博弈框架扩展到双目标热带伪线性优化，通过双参数(λ1, λ2)刻画Pareto前沿，证明其凸分段线性性质，给出联合分母界|Δ|≤2，并提出方向二分法(O(n²(n+m)log M))和牛顿方案（最多|S|步）两种算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将单参数热带伪线性优化推广到双目标情形，解决同时最小化两个热带伪线性目标的问题，并刻画其Pareto前沿的结构性质。

Method: 采用双参数(λ1, λ2)参数化平均收益博弈框架，分析可行性区域R和Pareto前沿P的凸分段线性性质；推导联合分母界；设计方向二分法和牛顿方案两种算法。

Result: 证明Pareto前沿是凸分段线性曲线且仅有有限个断点；给出联合分母界：对任意基本圈γ有k1(γ)+k2(γ)≤2，所有断点坐标为半整数；方向二分法复杂度O(n²(n+m)log M)，牛顿方案最多|S|步且与M无关。

Conclusion: 双目标热带伪线性优化的Pareto前沿具有与单参数情形自然兼容的凸分段线性结构，联合分母界是一个全新的理论结果；两种算法各有适用场景，数值实验验证了理论复杂度界。

Abstract: We extend the parametric mean-payoff game framework of Parsons et al. to bi-objective tropical pseudolinear optimization with general two-sided constraints. The problem is to simultaneously minimize two tropical pseudolinear objectives over the feasible set of a general two-sided system U otimes x oplus b is less than or equal to V otimes x oplus d, we characterize the Pareto front via a parametric mean-payoff game in two parameters ( lambda 1, lambda 2). The feasibility region R is convex and the Pareto front P is a convex piecewise-linear curve with finitely many breakpoints, these properties are natural extensions of the single-parameter case to two parameters. In addition, we give as a new result, the joint denominator bound: the cycle coefficients satisfy k 1( gamma ) + k 2( gamma ) is less than or equal to 2 for any elementary cycle gamma, yielding | Delta | is less than or equal to 2 for every 2 times 2 Newton system, except in the fully decoupled case, and implying that all breakpoints have half-integer coordinates for integer data. Optimality and infeasibility certificates are given in terms of the cycle structure of the parametric game. Two algorithms are developed, a directional bisection algorithm (O(n squared (n+m) log M) per direction) and a Newton scheme tracing the complete Pareto front via 2 times 2 linear solves in at most | S | steps, independent of M. The directional bisection algorithm is pseudo-polynomial in n, m and M. The Newton scheme is independent of M but requires up to |S| steps, where |S| is exponential in n. Lastly, we give numerical experiments on random instances to confirm the directional bisection complexity bound exactly; the Newton scheme's worst-case bound is not attained by random instances but is shown to be tight via explicit adversarial constructions.

</details>


### [107] [Fast and Scalable Caputo Fractional Gradient Descent via Perturbation-Preserving Memory Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15505)
*Hwanseo Lee,Junseo Lee,Hyunju Kim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出两种高效计算策略（SOE近似和DHDC压缩）来降低Caputo型分数阶梯度下降中历史依赖卷积的计算成本（从O(n²)降至近线性），并在理论上证明在近似误差可控下仍保持单调下降和线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶梯度下降虽能通过Caputo算子引入长程记忆来改善病态和非凸优化问题的稳定性，但其实际应用受限，主要原因是历史依赖卷积的计算成本随迭代次数呈二次增长（O(n²)），亟需降低计算开销而不破坏其记忆结构。

Method: 首先将分数阶下降方向表示为过去梯度的离散卷积，形成统一视角。然后提出两种互补机制：(1) 利用指数和（SOE）近似幂律核，实现高效递归更新；(2) 新提出的二元层次离散卷积（DHDC），通过多尺度聚合策略压缩梯度历史。并将这些近似视为理想Caputo算子的扰动来分析。

Result: 在μ-强凸和L-光滑假设下，证明了只要近似误差可控，压缩记忆后的方法仍保持单调下降和线性收敛。

Conclusion: 通过SOE和DHDC两种方法，Caputo型分数阶梯度下降可在保持记忆结构优势的同时大幅降低计算成本，从理论层面保证了计算可行性与优化性能的平衡。

Abstract: Fractional gradient descent (FGD) incorporates long-range memory through Caputo-type operators and has been shown to improve stability in ill-conditioned and nonconvex optimization problems. Despite these advantages, its practical use remains limited, mainly due to the high computational cost of evaluating history-dependent convolutions, which scales quadratically with the number of iterations. In this paper, we focus on making Caputo-based optimization computationally viable without sacrificing its intrinsic memory structure. We begin by expressing the fractional descent direction as a discrete convolution over past gradients, which provides a unified view of the method. Based on this formulation, we introduce two complementary mechanisms to reduce the cost of the memory term. The first uses a sum-of-exponentials (SOE) approximation of the power-law kernel, leading to efficient recursive updates. The second approach, newly proposed in this paper as dyadic hierarchical discrete convolution (DHDC), compresses the gradient history through a multiscale aggregation strategy. Rather than treating these approximations as purely numerical accelerations, we interpret them as perturbations of the ideal Caputo operator. This viewpoint allows us to analyze how the compressed memory affects the optimization dynamics. Under standard $μ$-strong convexity and $L$-smoothness assumptions, we show that the resulting method still exhibits monotone descent and linear convergence, provided that the approximation error remains controlled.

</details>


### [108] [Integer quadratic obstructions to the local controllability of the Burgers equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15735)
*Thomas Perrin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Burgers方程在标量控制作用于固定空间分布情况下的可控性问题。在线性化系统不可控的背景下，识别了导致二次阻碍小时间局部零可控性的自然条件，这些条件通过控制的任意整数阶负Sobolev范数量化。证明了这些条件与迭代李括号相关，并通过二次展开中的重复分部积分结合非线性余项估计完成了证明。最后，构造了在每个负整数阶都产生阻碍的光滑空间分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线性化系统不可控的奇异控制问题中，理解非线性效应如何影响可控性，特别是识别导致小时间局部零可控性二次阻碍的自然条件。

Method: 通过二次展开中的重复分部积分，结合非线性余项估计，利用迭代李括号条件分析控制分布对可控性的影响。

Result: 识别了导致二次阻碍的自然条件与迭代李括号相关；构造了在每个负整数阶Sobolev范数都产生阻碍的光滑空间分布。

Conclusion: 在Burgers方程中，即使线性化系统不可控，非线性二次效应仍可导致可控性的进一步阻碍，且这些阻碍可通过负Sobolev范数量化并与李括号结构相关。

Abstract: We consider the Burgers equation with a scalar control acting through a fixed spatial profile, in a regime where the linearized system is not controllable. We identify natural conditions on the control profile leading to quadratic obstructions to small-time local null-controllability, quantified by negative Sobolev norms of the control of arbitrary integer order. We show that these conditions are related to iterated Lie brackets. The proof relies on repeated integrations by parts in the quadratic expansion combined with nonlinear remainder estimates. Finally, we construct smooth spatial profiles that produce obstructions at every negative integer order.

</details>


### [109] [Polynomial-Based Solutions to Targeting Problems for Onboard Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15801)
*Adam Evans,Alberto Fossa,Roberto Armellin,Didier Henrion,Renato Zanetti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于矩-SOS优化的航天器目标瞄准方法，利用微分代数将轨迹优化转化为多项式优化问题，在保证全局最优收敛性的同时实现高精度计算，并扩展到地月系低推力连续站保持任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非线性规划求解目标瞄准问题存在收敛到局部最优的风险，而线性近似在大机动和长传播时间条件下精度迅速下降，亟需一种兼具高精度、计算效率和可靠收敛性的方法。

Method: 首先通过微分代数计算非线性动力学与约束的高阶泰勒展开，将轨迹优化问题重构为多项式优化问题（POP）；然后利用矩-SOS优化求解该POP；同时提出基于二阶动力学校核的凸优化方法作为对比。

Result: 矩-SOS方法在精度上与传统NLP相当，但在温和假设下保证全局最优收敛；在大机动和长传播时间条件下性能优于线性近似方法；成功扩展到地月系CR3BP中的连续低推力站保持问题，并在存在显著状态误差时进行了评估。

Conclusion: 矩-SOS方法能够直接处理非凸约束并将复杂非线性动力学转化为具有可靠收敛性的形式，适合自主星上应用。

Abstract: This paper solves the targeting problem focusing on accuracy, computational efficiency, and reliability. The trajectory optimization problem is first recast as a polynomial optimization problem (POP) by leveraging differential algebra to compute high-order Taylor expansions of the nonlinear dynamics and constraints. Moment-sum-of-squares (SOS) optimization is then utilized to solve this POP. A convex formulation based on a second-order expansion of the dynamics is also proposed. For impulsive targeting, the moment-SOS and convex approaches are compared against traditional nonlinear programming (NLP) solvers and map inversion techniques. Results indicate that the moment-SOS approach provides solutions as accurate as traditional NLP, but with the critical advantage of guaranteeing convergence to the global optimum under mild assumptions. Furthermore, the method excels at handling large maneuvers and long propagation times, conditions in which standard linear approximations rapidly degrade. To demonstrate its versatility, the methodology is extended to a continuous low-thrust station keeping (SK) scenario in the Earth-Moon Circular Restricted Three-Body Problem. The algorithm's performance is then evaluated in the presence of significant state errors. The ability to directly handle non-convex constraints and recast complex, nonlinear dynamics into formulations with reliable convergence properties makes the moment-SOS approach suitable for autonomous onboard applications.

</details>


### [110] [Analytical and Reduced-Order Modeling of a Timoshenko Beam with Point Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15802)
*Alexander Zuyev,Julia Kalosha*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于频域建模的受控机械系统（柔性梁+刚体）分析方法，利用Timoshenko方程解析推导了传递函数，并通过Loewner矩阵构建降阶模型，最后通过不同传感器-执行器配置的Bode图对比验证了框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对包含柔性梁与刚体的受控机械系统，现有建模方法在频域分析中缺乏精确且可降阶的解析框架，需要一种既能保留无限维系统动力学特性、又能实现高效降阶建模的方法。

Method: 1) 基于Timoshenko梁方程及体-梁界面条件，解析推导了系统的传递函数（无限维表示）；2) 利用Loewner矩阵框架构建降阶模型；3) 通过不同传感器-执行器配对下的Bode图对比进行验证。

Result: 成功推导了Timoshenko梁在体-梁耦合条件下的频域传递函数；Loewner矩阵降阶模型能够有效逼近无限维系统行为；不同传感器-执行器配置下的Bode图对比验证了所提频域建模框架的准确性。

Conclusion: 提出的频域建模框架能够准确描述柔性梁-刚体耦合系统的动力学特性，Loewner矩阵降阶方法为无限维系统的工程应用提供了可行的简化途径，且不同传感-执行配置下的验证进一步确认了模型的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present frequency-domain models of a controlled mechanical system consisting of a flexible beam and a rigid body. The transfer function for the beam, governed by the Timoshenko equations under the body-beam interface conditions, is derived analytically. Based on this infinite-dimensional representation, reduced-order models are constructed within the framework of Loewner matrices. A comparative analysis of the Bode plots is presented for the beam model using different choices of sensor-actuator pairs to validate the proposed frequency-domain modeling framework.

</details>


### [111] [Global solutions for the sensors placement problem via weakly convex optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15821)
*Giovanni Bruccola*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种在不了解底层动力学的情况下最优放置有限数量传感器以重构高维信号的方法，通过非凸组合优化问题的重新表述为弱凸约束投影问题，并采用精确切割球算法计算ε-全局解，同时提出逆向切割球算法来改进启发式解或验证其ε-全局最优性。在XFOIL数据的NACA翼型压力重建任务上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无法获取底层动力学先验知识的情况下，如何最优地放置有限数量的传感器以重构高维信号，这是一个具有挑战性的非凸组合优化问题。

Method: 将非凸组合优化问题重新表述为弱凸约束投影问题，采用Inexact Cutting Sphere算法计算ε-全局解；并提出Inverse Cutting Sphere算法，从任意可行启发式解出发，要么在给定容差ε内改进解，要么验证其ε-全局最优性。

Result: 在基于XFOIL数据的NACA翼型压力重建任务上进行了实验评估。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够有效解决传感器最优放置问题，逆向切割球算法同时具备解改进和最优性验证的能力。

Abstract: We address the problem of optimally placing a limited number of sensors to reconstruct high-dimensional signals without knowledge of the underlying dynamics. The task is formulated as a nonconvex combinatorial optimisation problem and recast as a weakly convex constrained projection problem. This reformulation allows us to compute $\varepsilon$-global solutions using the Inexact Cutting Sphere algorithm. We further propose the Inverse Cutting Sphere algorithm, which starts from any feasible heuristic solution and either improves it by a prescribed tolerance $\varepsilon$ or certifies its $\varepsilon$-global optimality. The framework is evaluated on pressure reconstruction for NACA airfoils using XFOIL data.

</details>


### [112] [Two-Thread Coverage MCTS for SAT and XSAT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15834)
*Marcel Crasmaru*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种结合WalkSAT风格rollout与双线程对称性初始化的MCTS求解器，用于SAT和XSAT问题，在多个基准测试中表现出色，包括100/100的SATLIB图着色编码、20/20的3-XOR-SAT实例，以及一个SAT Competition 2025实例，并在16轮DES密钥恢复编码中将负子句计数从约200降至24。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SAT求解器在处理某些结构化问题（如图着色、XOR约束、密码分析编码）时仍面临挑战，需要一种结合MCTS全局搜索能力与局部搜索效率的新方法。

Method: 采用Monte-Carlo Tree Search作为求解框架，核心创新包括：(1) WalkSAT风格的rollout策略进行局部搜索；(2) 双线程对称性破坏初始化，一个线程从全真开始，另一个从全假开始，每个线程到满足赋值的初始Hamming距离被限制在⌊n/2⌋以内。

Result: 在SATLIB图着色编码上100/100求解成功（毫秒级）；20/20的3-XOR-SAT实例在n=200时中位数10秒，n=300时中位数138秒；SAT Competition 2025实例Break-triple-04-06.xml.cnf在50毫秒内求解；16轮DES密钥恢复编码（n=1976, m=30072）在7小时内将负子句从约200降至24（99.9%子句满足）。

Conclusion: 该MCTS求解器结合对称性初始化和WalkSAT风格rollout能有效处理SAT和XSAT问题，极性分裂策略（双线程）本身就能解决某些实例，对于密码分析等大规模结构化问题也能显著减少未满足子句数量。

Abstract: We introduce a Monte-Carlo Tree Search solver for SAT and XSAT that pairs WalkSAT-style rollouts with a two-thread symmetry-breaking initialisation: one thread starts from all-true, the other from all-false, bounding each thread's initial Hamming distance to a satisfying assignment by $\lfloor n/2 \rfloor$. Empirically the solver closes 100/100 SATLIB graph-colouring encodings (flat200-479, sw100) in tens of milliseconds each, 20/20 planted 3-XOR-SAT at $n{=}200$ (median 10~s), 6/6 at $n{=}300$ (median 138~s), and one SAT Competition 2025 instance (Break-triple-04-06.xml.cnf) in 50~ms via the polarity split alone. On a full 16-round DES key-recovery encoding ($n{=}1976$, $m{=}30072$) it drives the negative-clause count from $\sim 200$ down to 24 (99.9% clauses satisfied) over 7 hours before hitting the S-box plateau.

</details>


### [113] [A Numerically-safe Branch-Price-and-Cut Algorithm for the Length-Constrained Cycle Partition Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15837)
*Mohammed Ghannam,Ambros Gleixner,Gioni Mexi,Edward Lam*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对长度约束的环分割问题（LCCP），提出了基于集合分割模型的精确分支定价切割算法，利用动态规划定价算法进行双向搜索和对称性破缺，显著优于现有方法，能求解多达76节点的问题，并闭合14个之前未解的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LCCP是图优化中的重要问题，现有方法的求解规模和效率有限，需要更精确高效的算法来获取数值安全的bound并解决更大规模的实例。

Method: 将LCCP建模为集合分割问题，采用精确的branch-price-and-cut方法；定价算法基于动态规划，利用双向搜索和对称性破缺高效生成改进环。

Result: LP松弛提供了非常强的dual bounds；新方法在求解时间上大幅优于现有方法；解决了之前52节点限制，最高求解到76节点；闭合了14个之前未解的实例并给出数值安全的bound。

Conclusion: 所提出的branch-price-and-cut方法显著提升了LCCP的求解能力，在效率和规模上均实现了突破。

Abstract: The length-constrained cycle partition problem (LCCP) is a graph optimization problem in which a set of nodes must be partitioned into a minimum number of cycles. Every node is associated with a critical time and the length of every cycle must not exceed the critical time of any node in the cycle. We formulate LCCP as a set partitioning model and solve it using an exact branch-price-and-cut approach. Our dynamic programming-based pricing algorithm to generate improving cycles exploits the particular structure of the pricing problem for efficient bidirectional search and symmetry breaking. Computational results show that the LP relaxation of the set partitioning model produces very strong dual bounds and our branch-price-and-cut method improves significantly over the state of the art. It is able to solve previously solved instances in a fraction of the time and closes 14 previously unsolved instances with numerically safe bounds, one of which has 76 nodes, a notable improvement over the previous limit of 52 nodes.

</details>


### [114] [PriEco-DRL: Joint Optimization of Electric-Bus Eco-Driving and Transit-Priority Adaptive Signals via Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15862)
*Dingshan Sun,Ang Li,Chaopeng Tan,Zicheng Su,Wanjing Ma,Marco Rinaldi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出PriEco-DRL框架，通过深度强化学习联合优化电动公交生态驾驶与公交优先自适应信号控制，在真实走廊实验中相比基线方法降低了电动公交能耗，同时保持了网络效率和公交优先性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市公交电气化需要在电动公交（EB）的能量效率、时刻表可靠性和乘坐舒适性之间取得平衡，特别是在拥堵网络中与公交优先自适应信号交互时存在挑战。

Method: 提出PriEco-DRL联合优化框架，信号层采用优先级加权最大压力（Priority-MP）控制器根据占用率感知压力分配绿灯时间，车辆层基于不确定的动态局部信号线索自适应纵向控制；采用集中训练分散执行（CTDE）和参数共享，单个DRL智能体从多条公交线路学习。

Result: 在真实走廊实验中，与固定时间、感应式和基于规则的信号-车辆协调基线相比，PriEco-DRL降低了EB能耗并保持了网络效率和公交优先性；改进源于更少的非计划停-启事件和更平滑的速度调节。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了可调的能量-时间权衡，通过奖励加权实现灵活的操作选择。

Abstract: Urban transit electrification requires balancing energy efficiency, schedule reliability, and ride comfort for electric buses (EBs), particularly when interacting with transit-priority adaptive signals in congested networks. This paper proposes PriEco-DRL, a joint optimization framework that integrates EB eco-driving with transit-priority adaptive signal control using deep reinforcement learning (DRL). The signal layer employs a priority-weighted max-pressure (Priority-MP) controller to allocate green time based on occupancy-aware pressures, while the vehicle layer adapts longitudinal control based on uncertain and dynamically evolving local signal cues. A structured reward combines guidance and event-based reinforcement to align EB arrivals with green opportunities while considering energy, time, comfort, and safety. The framework uses centralized training and decentralized execution (CTDE) with parameter sharing, allowing a single DRL agent to learn from multiple buses and routes using local observations. Experiments on a real-world corridor show that PriEco-DRL reduces EB energy consumption while maintaining network efficiency and transit priority compared with fixed-time, actuated, and rule-based signal-vehicle coordination baselines. Energy- and trajectory-based analyses reveal that the improvements stem from fewer unscheduled stop-start events and smoother speed regulation under adaptive signals. The results highlight a tunable energy-time trade-off, allowing flexible operational choices through reward weighting.

</details>


### [115] [An Improved Analysis of the Last Iterate of the SubGradient Method in the Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15980)
*Guglielmo Beretta,Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni,Andrea Paudice*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在二维空间中，使用常步长η=Θ(1/√n)的次梯度方法(sGM)在最后迭代点上可实现O(1/√n)的优化误差，消除了高维情况下出现的log n因子，部分解决了Koren和Segal于2020年提出的关于固定维度下sGM最后迭代最优误差的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Koren和Segal在2020年提出的关于固定维度下次梯度方法(sGM)最后迭代最优误差的开放问题，探究二维情形下是否能消除高维中出现的额外log n因子。

Method: 针对定义在R²上的凸Lipschitz目标函数，采用常步长η=Θ(1/√n)的次梯度方法(sGM)，分析有限时间域n内最后迭代点的优化误差界。

Result: 证明在二维平面中，最后迭代点的优化误差为O(1/√n)，优于高维情形下包含log n因子的误差界。

Conclusion: 二维空间中sGM的最后迭代点无需额外的log n因子即可达到最优收敛率，在固定维度问题上取得了理论进展。

Abstract: We study the last iterate of the subGradient Method (sGM) for convex Lipschitz objectives defined on $\mathbb R^2$. We prove that for a finite horizon $n$ and a constant stepsize $η= Θ(1/\sqrt{n})$, the last iterate achieves an optimization error of order $1/\sqrt{n}$, showing that the extra $\log{n}$ factor appearing in high dimensions is unnecessary in the plane. This makes progress on an open problem posed by Koren and Segal in 2020 on the optimal error of the last iterate of sGM in fixed dimension.

</details>


### [116] [Optimal control of symmetry-breaking dynamics near criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16188)
*Pearson W. Miller*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究动力系统在叉形分岔附近的最优控制问题，通过庞特里亚金最大值原理的渐近展开，分析了三种控制强度标度下的最优控制律，推导了弱控和中等控制区的广义振幅方程，并在弱控区解析了分岔结构和长时间渐近解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受细胞命运选择等自然过程中外部线索引导对称性破缺转变的启发，研究动力系统在叉形分岔附近的最优控制问题。

Method: 利用庞特里亚金最大值原理对最优性条件进行渐近展开，分析一般n维系统的领先阶最优控制律；推导弱控和中等控制区的广义振幅方程；通过边界层方法构建长时间渐近解；在双稳态生化开关规范模型上进行数值验证。

Result: 强控极限下结果退化为线性二次型控制的已知近似；推导了弱控和中等控制区状态与共态变量共演的广义振幅方程（控制规范形）；解析了弱控区最优控制问题的分岔结构；利用边界层方法构建了该区长时渐近解。

Conclusion: 建立了叉形分岔附近动力系统最优控制的渐近理论框架，揭示了不同控制强度标度下的控制律结构，并通过数值验证了理论有效性。

Abstract: We study the problem of optimal control for dynamical systems near a pitchfork bifurcation, motivated by the role of external cues in guiding symmetry-breaking transitions in cell-fate selection and other natural processes. Using an asymptotic expansion of the optimality conditions obtained from the Pontryagin maximum principle, the leading-order optimal control law for a general n-dimensional system is examined across three dynamical regimes distinguished by scaling of control strength with respect to the distance from criticality. While in the strong control limit the results reduce to known approximations from linear-quadratic control, we derive generalized amplitude equations for the co-evolution of state and costate variables describing the optimized trajectory in the weak and intermediate control regimes. These control normal forms are validated against numerical solutions of the full optimal control problem for a canonical model of a bistable biochemical switch. The bifurcation structure of the optimal control problem is analyzed in the weak control regime. Finally, we demonstrate the construction of asymptotic solutions in the long time limit in this regime using boundary-layer methods.

</details>
